  Go Actor Pattern + gcdeadtrace Demo
========================================
  Runtime: go1.27-devel_6eb1c990cb Mon Jul 13 17:38:21 2026 +0800 windows/amd64

  Workers:
    worker-loop        — loop alloc, all die
    worker-mixed       — multi-site, some live/some die
    worker-session     — single session tracking
    worker-fullydead   — per-site fully-dead detection
    worker-concurrent  — two concurrent sessions, same site
    worker-customtypes — struct, map, interface, string types
